All access goes through the persistence library. |\n| CLI-004 | Document every command's usage | Each command accepts `--help`, prints its usage and a one-line summary to standard output, and exits 0. |\n| CLI-005 | Run the test suite offline | `bin/test.sh` runs the pytest suite with network access denied and exits zero only when every test passes. |\n\n### Feature: ECB History Loading\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| LOAD-001 | Validate an ECB history file before writing | The header, field counts, real and unique dates, and value rules are checked over the whole file. Any violation exits 3 and writes nothing. |\n| LOAD-002 | Store published rates as text | Each non-N/A rate is stored as published text, replacing any existing row for the same date and currency. The `dates,rates` counts are reported, and reloading leaves data unchanged. |\n| LOAD-003 | Report an unreadable file | A missing or unreadable file exits 1 with one stderr line and writes nothing. |\n| LOAD-004 | Make loads visible to a running server | A load completed while the server runs is visible to the next request. |\n| LOAD-005 | Fetch and load the ECB archive | `fetch` downloads the ECB zip, extracts `eurofxref-hist.csv`, and loads it. A failed download exits 1. Tests stub the download. |\n\n### Feature: Rate Lookup\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| RATES-001 | Serve the API and report health | `fxrates serve --port n` listens on 127.0.0.1 (default 8000), and `GET /health` returns 200 with `{\"status\": \"ok\"}`. |\n| RATES-002 | Report store statistics | `GET /stats` returns `dates`, `rates`, `first`, and `last`, with `first` and `last` null when the table is empty. |\n| RATES-003 | List stored currencies | `GET /currencies` returns base EUR and the ascending codes that have a stored rate. |\n| RATES-004 | Resolve rates for a date | `GET /rates/{date}` uses the latest publication on or before the date. It returns 404 when none exists and 422 for a malformed date. |\n| RATES-005 | Report the latest rates | `GET /rates/latest` returns the latest publication with `\"requested\": \"latest\"`. |\n\n### Feature: Conversion\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CONVERT-001 | Convert an amount between currencies | `GET /convert` returns the effective date, a six-decimal rate, and a two-decimal result, computed exactly and rounded half to even. EUR has the implicit rate 1. The latest publication is used when `date` is omitted. |\n| CONVERT-002 | Refuse unknown currencies and bad inputs | An unknown currency, or no rate on the effective date, returns 404. A missing or malformed `amount`, `date`, or required parameter returns 422. |\n\n### Feature: Currency History\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| HIST-001 | List a currency's points over a period | `GET /history/{code}` returns ascending publication points from `start` through `end` inclusive. Both bounds are optional. |\n| HIST-002 | Summarize a currency's history | `count`, `min`, and `max` (stored text), and `mean` (six decimals, half to even) are returned. An empty history yields `count` 0 and null statistics. |\n| HIST-003 | Refuse unknown currencies and bad bounds | A currency with no stored rate, including EUR, returns 404. A malformed `start` or `end` returns 422. |\n\n### Feature: Acceptance\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| ACCEPT-001 | Supplied grader passes end to end | `sh sources/run.sh` exits zero, and every supplied check passes. |\n\n## Surfaced Acceptance Criteria\n\n| ID | Story ID | Criterion |\n|---|---|---|\n| AC-001 | CLI-004 | Each command accepts `--help`, prints its usage and a one-line summary to standard output, and exits 0. |\n| AC-002 | CLI-001 | `serve --port` with a value outside 1 to 65535 exits 2 with one stderr line and binds no socket. |\n| AC-003 | RATES-001 | If the requested port is already in use, then `serve` exits 1 with one stderr line and answers no requests. |\n| AC-004 | RATES-001 | When `serve` receives SIGTERM, the process stops and exits 0. |\n| AC-005 | RATES-001 | The FastAPI-generated OpenAPI description is served at `/openapi.json`, and its interactive reference at `/docs`. |\n| AC-006 | RATES-003 | When no rate is stored, `GET /currencies` returns base EUR with an empty `currencies` list. |\n| AC-007 | RATES-005 | If no publication is stored, then `GET /rates/latest` responds 404. |\n| AC-008 | CONVERT-001 | When `from` and `to` name the same currency, the rate is `1.000000` and the result is the amount rounded half to even to two decimals. |\n| AC-009 | CONVERT-001 | When `amount` is `0`, the result is `0.00`. |\n| AC-010 | LOAD-002 | When a file holds only N/A rates, `load` prints `dates,rates` then `0,0` and stores nothing. |\n| AC-011 | LOAD-004 | A read request sees the rates from before a load or from after it, never a partly applied load. |\n| AC-012 | HIST-001 | When `start` is later than `end`, `GET /history/{code}` returns 200 with `count` 0 and `points` `[]`. |\n| AC-013 | LOAD-005 | If the downloaded archive does not contain `eurofxref-hist.csv`, then `fetch` exits 1 and stores nothing. |\n\n## Relationship Model\n\n| Source or group | Relationship type | Related source or group | context | stage |\n\n## Planning Instructions\n\n### Delivery Shape\n\nThe inferred system is a command line (`fxrates`) plus a FastAPI JSON API served by uvicorn, over one SQLite table named `rates`. The project type is `ambiguous`: it has described commands and described HTTP endpoints with no screens. Inputs are ECB history CSV files and, for `fetch`, the ECB zip archive. Outputs are stdout counts for `load`, JSON responses for the API, and exit codes throughout. The required flow is `init`, then `load`, then `serve`, with loads allowed while serving.\n\n### Story Realization Map\n\n- CLI-001: capability. Scope: shared argument parsing and exit codes 2. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Exit codes; sources/run.sh U1–U4.\n- CLI-002: capability. Scope: `init` drops and recreates `rates`. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Command line; sources/run.sh A1.\n- CLI-003: integration. Scope: `DATABASE_URL` resolution through the persistence library. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Database; sources/run.sh sets `DATABASE_URL`.\n- CLI-004: capability. Scope: per-command `--help`. Evidence: Core Baseline (cli); sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Command line.\n- CLI-005: test harness. Scope: `bin/test.sh` runs pytest offline. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Tests.\n- LOAD-001: capability. Scope: full-file validation. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §ECB history rules 1–3; sources/eurofxref-hist_bad.csv; sources/run.sh A4.\n- LOAD-002: capability. Scope: store, replace, and count. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §ECB history rules 2, 4, 5; sources/run.sh A2, A3.\n- LOAD-003: capability. Scope: unreadable file exits 1. Evidence: sources/run.sh A5.\n- LOAD-004: acceptance contract. Scope: loads while serving. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §HTTP API last paragraph; sources/run.sh L1–L5.\n- LOAD-005: integration. Scope: ECB zip download, extraction, and load, with a stubbed download in tests. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Command line `fetch`.\n- RATES-001: capability. Scope: `serve --port`, 127.0.0.1 binding, and `/health`. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Command line; sources/run.sh `start_server`, H1.\n- RATES-002: capability. Scope: `/stats`. Evidence: sources/run.sh H2, L2.\n- RATES-003: capability. Scope: `/currencies`. Evidence: sources/run.sh H3.\n- RATES-004: capability. Scope: effective-date resolution and `/rates/{date}`. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Effective date; sources/run.sh H4–H7, L4.\n- RATES-005: capability. Scope: `/rates/latest`. Evidence: sources/run.sh H8, L3.\n- CONVERT-001: capability. Scope: `/convert` exact arithmetic and rounding. Evidence: sources/run.sh H9–H12, H11 (default latest).\n- CONVERT-002: capability. Scope: `/convert` error statuses. Evidence: sources/run.sh H13–H16.\n- HIST-001: capability. Scope: `/history/{code}` points and range. Evidence: sources/run.sh H17, H18, H20.\n- HIST-002: capability. Scope: `/history` statistics. Evidence: sources/run.sh H17, H18, L5.\n- HIST-003: capability. Scope: `/history` error statuses. Evidence: sources/run.sh H19, H21.\n- ACCEPT-001: acceptance contract. Scope: the supplied grader end to end. Evidence: sources/run.sh.\n\n### Test and Acceptance Strategy\n\nFeature stories prove their own behavior with pytest against a real temporary SQLite database. The API tests use FastAPI's `TestClient`, and `fetch` tests use a stubbed download. Each feature story declares `Suite: scoped` and runs only its own pytest selector. `sources/run.sh` has no scope selector, so no feature story runs it.\n\n`ACCEPT-001` is the single terminal verification story. It depends on all 20 other stories, and its acceptance assertion declares `Suite: full`. It runs `sh sources/run.sh` unfiltered, and its verdict is that script's exit status. Sea Trial `st-001` is the matching proof.\n\n### Sequencing and Dependencies\n\n- CLI-001 and CLI-003 precede every command story.\n- CLI-002 precedes LOAD-001, and LOAD-001 precedes LOAD-002.\n- LOAD-002 precedes LOAD-003, LOAD-004, LOAD-005, HIST-001, and every rate-reading story.\n- RATES-001 precedes RATES-002 through RATES-005 and LOAD-004.\n- RATES-004 precedes CONVERT-001 and HIST-001.\n- CONVERT-001 precedes CONVERT-002. HIST-001 precedes HIST-002 and HIST-003.\n- CLI-004 follows the commands it documents.\n- ACCEPT-001 follows all other stories.\n- External dependency: none at runtime. `fetch` contacts the ECB only in production use.\n\n### Source Conflicts and Gaps\n\n**Cross-source conflicts.** None found. The brief and `run.sh` agree on every checked known answer, including the rounding examples in H9–H12, H17, H18, and L5. No conflicting definitions require a decision.\n\n**Selected behaviors (unattended resolution).** These are the narrowest behaviors consistent with the sources, and they are the planning baseline:\n\n1. `/rates` maps list only stored currencies. EUR is not listed, matching H4 and H8. EUR appears only as the implicit rate 1 in `/convert`.\n2. `/convert` with the same `from` and `to` returns rate `1.000000` (AC-008).\n3. `/history` with `start` after `end` returns 200 with an empty result (AC-012).\n4. A syntactically valid but impossible date such as `2025-13-01` counts as malformed and returns 422 (H7).\n5. `/rates/latest` is matched before `/rates/{date}`, so `latest` is not parsed as a malformed date.\n6. `--port` must be an integer from 1 to 65535. Otherwise the command exits 2.\n7. The API has no authentication. It serves public ECB reference rates, binds only to 127.0.0.1, and has no protected resource.\n8. A load is applied as one transaction, so readers never see a partial load (AC-011).\n9. `fetch` bounds its download with a timeout chosen by the builder. The sources state no timeout value, so none is invented. A failed or timed-out download exits 1 and stores nothing (st-013).\n10. Deployment is a local foreground process started by `./fxrates serve`. A SIGTERM stops it and exits 0 (AC-004).\n11. Sensitive data: none. ECB euro reference rates are public.\n12. Schema evolution: none. `init` builds the single table once, so migrations are not used within a release.\n\n**Gaps.** No Gap Checklist item requires a human decision. The Core Baseline gaps (help text and an API reference entry point) are covered by AC-001 and AC-005. Success criteria are not stated in the sources. The supplied grader is the acceptance verdict (st-001).\n\n## Analysis Notes\n\ngenerated: 2026-10-27\nblueprint: /mnt/c/Users/barlo/projects/drydock/uat/FxRates/runs/20261027.152427/workspace/targets/FxRates/blueprint\n\nQuality: Ready\n  blockers: 0\n  questions: 0\n  features: 6\n  stories: 21\n  stack: Python 3.11+, FastAPI, uvicorn, SQLite, pytest\n  display_name: not proposed\n  short_description: not proposed\n\nProject type is `ambiguous`: described commands and described HTTP endpoints. BEGIN ARTIFACT ANALYSIS.md ===\n# Blueprint Analysis: drydock FxRates Uat Case\n\n## Commander Expectations\n\n- assert the product loads ECB euro reference rate history files into a local SQLite database through a command line\n- assert the product serves rates by date, currency conversion, and currency history as JSON over HTTP on 127.0.0.1\n- assert every rate is stored and returned as published text, never as floating point\n- assert every conversion and statistic uses exact decimal arithmetic rounded half to even\n- assert the supplied grader `sources/run.sh` exits zero\n\n## Crew\n\n| Crew | Charge |\n|---|---|\n| Commander | Defines intent and decides what done means. |\n| Team Lead | Confirms epic completeness and stakeholder expectations. |\n| Planning Crew | Authors atomic specifications and the ordered Manifest. |\n| Shipyard Crew | Builds the tickets without synchronous Commander access. |\n\n## Story List\n\n### Feature: Command Line Foundation\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CLI-001 | Report usage errors with exit 2 | An unknown command, a wrong argument count, and a non-integer `--port` each exit 2 with empty stdout and one stderr line. |\n| CLI-002 | Initialize an empty rates table | `fxrates init` drops and recreates the `rates` table, prints nothing, and exits 0. |\n| CLI-003 | Select the database by environment | `DATABASE_URL` (optional `sqlite://` prefix), then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`. All access goes through the persistence library. |\n| CLI-004 | Document every command's usage | Each command accepts `--help`, prints its usage and a one-line summary to standard output, and exits 0. |\n| CLI-005 | Run the test suite offline | `bin/test.sh` runs the pytest suite with network access denied and exits zero only when every test passes. |\n\n### Feature: ECB History Loading\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| LOAD-001 | Validate an ECB history file before writing | The header, field counts, real and unique dates, and value rules are checked over the whole file. Any violation exits 3 and writes nothing. |\n| LOAD-002 | Store published rates as text | Each non-N/A rate is stored as published text, replacing any existing row for the same date and currency. The `dates,rates` counts are reported, and reloading leaves data unchanged. |\n| LOAD-003 | Report an unreadable file | A missing or unreadable file exits 1 with one stderr line and writes nothing. |\n| LOAD-004 | Make loads visible to a running server | A load completed while the server runs is visible to the next request. |\n| LOAD-005 | Fetch and load the ECB archive | `fetch` downloads the ECB zip, extracts `eurofxref-hist.csv`, and loads it. A failed download exits 1. Tests stub the download. |\n\n### Feature: Rate Lookup\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| RATES-001 | Serve the API and report health | `fxrates serve --port n` listens on 127.0.0.1 (default 8000), and `GET /health` returns 200 with `{\"status\": \"ok\"}`. |\n| RATES-002 | Report store statistics | `GET /stats` returns `dates`, `rates`, `first`, and `last`, with `first` and `last` null when the table is empty. |\n| RATES-003 | List stored currencies | `GET /currencies` returns base EUR and the ascending codes that have a stored rate. |\n| RATES-004 | Resolve rates for a date | `GET /rates/{date}` uses the latest publication on or before the date. It returns 404 when none exists and 422 for a malformed date. |\n| RATES-005 | Report the latest rates | `GET /rates/latest` returns the latest publication with `\"requested\": \"latest\"`. |\n\n### Feature: Conversion\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CONVERT-001 | Convert an amount between currencies | `GET /convert` returns the effective date, a six-decimal rate, and a two-decimal result, computed exactly and rounded half to even. EUR has the implicit rate 1. The latest publication is used when `date` is omitted. |\n| CONVERT-002 | Refuse unknown currencies and bad inputs | An unknown currency, or no rate on the effective date, returns 404. A missing or malformed `amount`, `date`, or required parameter returns 422. |\n\n### Feature: Currency History\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| HIST-001 | List a currency's points over a period | `GET /history/{code}` returns ascending publication points from `start` through `end` inclusive. Both bounds are optional. |\n| HIST-002 | Summarize a currency's history | `count`, `min`, and `max` (stored text), and `mean` (six decimals, half to even) are returned. An empty history yields `count` 0 and null statistics. |\n| HIST-003 | Refuse unknown currencies and bad bounds | A currency with no stored rate, including EUR, returns 404. A malformed `start` or `end` returns 422. |\n\n### Feature: Acceptance\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| ACCEPT-001 | Supplied grader passes end to end | `sh sources/run.sh` exits zero, and every supplied check passes. |\n\n## Surfaced Acceptance Criteria\n\n| ID | Story ID | Criterion |\n|---|---|---|\n| AC-001 | CLI-004 | Each command accepts `--help`, prints its usage and a one-line summary to standard output, and exits 0. |\n| AC-002 | CLI-001 | `serve --port` with a value outside 1 to 65535 exits 2 with one stderr line and binds no socket. |\n| AC-003 | RATES-001 | If the requested port is already in use, then `serve` exits 1 with one stderr line and answers no requests. |\n| AC-004 | RATES-001 | When `serve` receives SIGTERM, the process stops and exits 0. |\n| AC-005 | RATES-001 | The FastAPI-generated OpenAPI description is served at `/openapi.json`, and its interactive reference at `/docs`. |\n| AC-006 | RATES-003 | When no rate is stored, `GET /currencies` returns base EUR with an empty `currencies` list. |\n| AC-007 | RATES-005 | If no publication is stored, then `GET /rates/latest` responds 404. |\n| AC-008 | CONVERT-001 | When `from` and `to` name the same currency, the rate is `1.000000` and the result is the amount rounded half to even to two decimals. |\n| AC-009 | CONVERT-001 | When `amount` is `0`, the result is `0.00`. |\n| AC-010 | LOAD-002 | When a file holds only N/A rates, `load` prints `dates,rates` then `0,0` and stores nothing. |\n| AC-011 | LOAD-004 | A read request sees the rates from before a load or from after it, never a partly applied load. |\n| AC-012 | HIST-001 | When `start` is later than `end`, `GET /history/{code}` returns 200 with `count` 0 and `points` `[]`. |\n| AC-013 | LOAD-005 | If the downloaded archive does not contain `eurofxref-hist.csv`, then `fetch` exits 1 and stores nothing. |\n\n## Relationship Model\n\n| Source or group | Relationship type | Related source or group | Evidence | Delivery implication |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md (HTTP API) | instruction-to-test | sources/run.sh H1–H21, L1–L5 | Endpoint bodies and statuses match the known answers | HTTP stories are proven by the supplied grader and their own scoped tests |\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md (Command line, ECB history rules) | instruction-to-test | sources/run.sh A1–A5, U1–U4 | Exit codes and stdout counts | CLI and LOAD stories share exit-code behavior |\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md (ECB history rules 2–5) | parser-to-normalizer | Rate storage in the rates table | Rule 3 requires full validation before any write | LOAD-001 must complete before LOAD-002 |\n| sources/eurofxref-hist_2025-01.csv | dependency | sources/run.sh A2, A3, H2–H18, H19 | Known answers are computed from this file | Must be staged for the grader and for scoped tests |\n| sources/eurofxref-hist_2025-02-03_04.csv | dependency | sources/run.sh L1–L5 | Live-load checks append these dates | LOAD-004 depends on this fixture |\n| sources/eurofxref-hist_bad.csv | dependency | sources/run.sh A4 | Invalid value on the second row | LOAD-001 rejection rule |\n| sources/run.sh | dependency | sources/eurofxref-hist_*.csv | `DATA` resolves to the sibling directory of the CSV files | All three CSVs must be staged beside `run.sh` in `sources/` |\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md (Database) | dependency | Persistence library and generated relational layer | Only table `rates` with unique (`date`, `currency`) | CLI-003 and LOAD-002 share one schema |\n\n## Source Roles\n\n| Path | Role | Plan disposition | Build disposition |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md | author intent | compass | prompt-only |\n| sources/run.sh | conformance harness | context | stage |\n| sources/eurofxref-hist_2025-01.csv | fixture data | context | stage |\n| sources/eurofxref-hist_2025-02-03_04.csv | fixture data | context | stage |\n| sources/eurofxref-hist_bad.csv | fixture data | context | stage |\n\n## Planning Instructions\n\n### Delivery Shape\n\nThe inferred system is a command line (`fxrates`) plus a FastAPI JSON API served by uvicorn, over one SQLite table named `rates`. The project type is `ambiguous`: it has described commands and described HTTP endpoints with no screens. Inputs are ECB history CSV files and, for `fetch`, the ECB zip archive. Outputs are stdout counts for `load`, JSON responses for the API, and exit codes throughout. The required flow is `init`, then `load`, then `serve`, with loads allowed while serving.\n\n### Story Realization Map\n\n- CLI-001: capability. Scope: shared argument parsing and exit codes 2. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Exit codes; sources/run.sh U1–U4.\n- CLI-002: capability. Scope: `init` drops and recreates `rates`. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Command line; sources/run.sh A1.\n- CLI-003: integration. Scope: `DATABASE_URL` resolution through the persistence library. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Database; sources/run.sh sets `DATABASE_URL`.\n- CLI-004: capability. Scope: per-command `--help`. Evidence: Core Baseline (cli); sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Command line.\n- CLI-005: test harness. Scope: `bin/test.sh` runs pytest offline. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Tests.\n- LOAD-001: capability. Scope: full-file validation. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §ECB history rules 1–3; sources/eurofxref-hist_bad.csv; sources/run.sh A4.\n- LOAD-002: capability. Scope: store, replace, and count. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §ECB history rules 2, 4, 5; sources/run.sh A2, A3.\n- LOAD-003: capability. Scope: unreadable file exits 1. Evidence: sources/run.sh A5.\n- LOAD-004: acceptance contract. Scope: loads while serving. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §HTTP API last paragraph; sources/run.sh L1–L5.\n- LOAD-005: integration. Scope: ECB zip download, extraction, and load, with a stubbed download in tests. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Command line `fetch`.\n- RATES-001: capability. Scope: `serve --port`, 127.0.0.1 binding, and `/health`. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Command line; sources/run.sh `start_server`, H1.\n- RATES-002: capability. Scope: `/stats`. Evidence: sources/run.sh H2, L2.\n- RATES-003: capability. Scope: `/currencies`. Evidence: sources/run.sh H3.\n- RATES-004: capability. Scope: effective-date resolution and `/rates/{date}`. Evidence: sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md §Effective date; sources/run.sh H4–H7, L4.\n- RATES-005: capability. Scope: `/rates/latest`. Evidence: sources/run.sh H8, L3.\n- CONVERT-001: capability. Scope: `/convert` exact arithmetic and rounding. Evidence: sources/run.sh H9–H12, H11 (default latest).\n- CONVERT-002: capability. Scope: `/convert` error statuses. Evidence: sources/run.sh H13–H16.\n- HIST-001: capability. Scope: `/history/{code}` points and range. Evidence: sources/run.sh H17, H18, H20.\n- HIST-002: capability. Scope: `/history` statistics. Evidence: sources/run.sh H17, H18, L5.\n- HIST-003: capability. Scope: `/history` error statuses. Evidence: sources/run.sh H19, H21.\n- ACCEPT-001: acceptance contract. Scope: the supplied grader end to end. Evidence: sources/run.sh.\n\n### Test and Acceptance Strategy\n\nFeature stories prove their own behavior with pytest against a real temporary SQLite database. The API tests use FastAPI's `TestClient`, and `fetch` tests use a stubbed download. Each feature story declares `Suite: scoped` and runs only its own pytest selector. `sources/run.sh` has no scope selector, so no feature story runs it.\n\n`ACCEPT-001` is the single terminal verification story. It depends on all 20 other stories, and its acceptance assertion declares `Suite: full`. It runs `sh sources/run.sh` unfiltered, and its verdict is that script's exit status. Sea Trial `st-001` is the matching proof.\n\n### Sequencing and Dependencies\n\n- CLI-001 and CLI-003 precede every command story.\n- CLI-002 precedes LOAD-001, and LOAD-001 precedes LOAD-002.\n- LOAD-002 precedes LOAD-003, LOAD-004, LOAD-005, HIST-001, and every rate-reading story.\n- RATES-001 precedes RATES-002 through RATES-005 and LOAD-004.\n- RATES-004 precedes CONVERT-001 and HIST-001.\n- CONVERT-001 precedes CONVERT-002. HIST-001 precedes HIST-002 and HIST-003.\n- CLI-004 follows the commands it documents.\n- ACCEPT-001 follows all other stories.\n- External dependency: none at runtime. `fetch` contacts the ECB only in production use.\n\n### Source Conflicts and Gaps\n\n**Cross-source conflicts.** None found. The brief and `run.sh` agree on every checked known answer, including the rounding examples in H9–H12, H17, H18, and L5. No conflicting definitions require a decision.\n\n**Selected behaviors (unattended resolution).** These are the narrowest behaviors consistent with the sources, and they are the planning baseline:\n\n1. `/rates` maps list only stored currencies. EUR is not listed, matching H4 and H8. EUR appears only as the implicit rate 1 in `/convert`.\n2. `/convert` with the same `from` and `to` returns rate `1.000000` (AC-008).\n3. `/history` with `start` after `end` returns 200 with an empty result (AC-012).\n4. A syntactically valid but impossible date such as `2025-13-01` counts as malformed and returns 422 (H7).\n5. `/rates/latest` is matched before `/rates/{date}`, so `latest` is not parsed as a malformed date.\n6. `--port` must be an integer from 1 to 65535. Otherwise the command exits 2.\n7. The API has no authentication. It serves public ECB reference rates, binds only to 127.0.0.1, and has no protected resource.\n8. A load is applied as one transaction, so readers never see a partial load (AC-011).\n9. `fetch` bounds its download with a timeout chosen by the builder. The sources state no timeout value, so none is invented. A failed or timed-out download exits 1 and stores nothing (st-013).\n10. Deployment is a local foreground process started by `./fxrates serve`. A SIGTERM stops it and exits 0 (AC-004).\n11. Sensitive data: none. ECB euro reference rates are public.\n12. Schema evolution: none. `init` builds the single table once, so migrations are not used within a release.\n\n**Gaps.** No Gap Checklist item requires a human decision. The Core Baseline gaps (help text and an API reference entry point) are covered by AC-001 and AC-005. Success criteria are not stated in the sources. The supplied grader is the acceptance verdict (st-001).\n\n## Analysis Notes\n\ngenerated: 2026-10-27\nblueprint: /mnt/c/Users/barlo/projects/drydock/uat/FxRates/runs/20261027.152427/workspace/targets/FxRates/blueprint\n\nQuality: Ready\n  blockers: 0\n  questions: 0\n  features: 6\n  stories: 21\n  stack: Python 3.11+, FastAPI, uvicorn, SQLite, pytest\n  display_name: not proposed\n  short_description: not proposed\n\nProject type is `ambiguous`: described commands and described HTTP endpoints. answers in `sources/run.sh`.\nVerification: proof\nPattern: ubiquitous\n\n## st-007: Invalid requests are refused with the right status\n\nType: behavioral\nRequired: yes\nCriterion: If a request has a malformed date or amount or lacks a required parameter, then the application shall respond 422; if it names an unknown currency, then it shall respond 404.\nVerification: proof\nPattern: unwanted\n\n## st-008: Persistence goes through the shipped library\n\nType: technical\nRequired: yes\nCriterion: The application shall reach the database only through the shipped persistence library, selected by `DATABASE_URL`, with rates stored as text and never as floating point.\nVerification: llm\nPattern: ubiquitous\n\n## st-009: Tests run offline\n\nType: technical\nRequired: yes\nCriterion: The application's `bin/test.sh` shall exit zero when run with network access denied.\nVerification: proof\nPattern: ubiquitous\nCommand: [\"unshare\", \"-rn\", \"sh\", \"bin/test.sh\"]\n\n## st-010: Fetch is tested through a stubbed download\n\nType: technical\nRequired: yes\nCriterion: The application's automated tests shall run `fetch` against a stubbed download, covering a successful load and a failed download that exits 1.\nVerification: llm\nPattern: ubiquitous\n\n## st-011: The service binds only to loopback\n\nType: guardrail\nRequired: yes\nCriterion: The fxrates application shall never listen on any address other than 127.0.0.1.\nVerification: llm\nPattern: ubiquitous\n\n## st-012: No network use beyond the ECB download\n\nType: guardrail\nRequired: yes\nCriterion: The fxrates application shall never open a network connection except the ECB archive download performed by `fetch`.\nVerification: llm\nPattern: ubiquitous\n\n## st-013: A failed fetch stores nothing\n\nType: behavioral\nRequired: yes\nCriterion: If the ECB download fails or times out, then the `fxrates fetch` command shall exit 1 and store nothing.\nVerification: proof\nPattern: unwanted\n\n## st-014: The grader is never read by the application\n\nType: guardrail\nRequired: yes\nCriterion: The fxrates application shall never read `sources/run.sh` or embed any known answer from it.\nVerification: llm\nPattern: ubiquitous\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md ===\n# Technology Stack\n\n| Technology | Rigging | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| Python
